&-\frac{i}{384}\rho^{-2}Y{}_{i}{}_{j}(\gamma{}_{a}{}_{b}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\beta}\epsilon{}_{c}{}^{a}{}^{b}{}^{d}{}^{e}\big(\mathcal D^{c}R(Q){}_{d}{}_{e}{}^{\alpha}{}^{j}\big)\lambda{}_{\beta}{}^{i} -\frac{1}{36}\rho^{-2}Y{}_{i}{}_{j}(\gamma{}^{a}){}^{\alpha}{}^{\beta}(\gamma{}_{b}{}_{a}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\gamma}\big(\mathcal D^{b}\chi{}_{\beta}{}^{j}\big)\lambda{}_{\gamma}{}^{i}\\
&-\frac{1}{36}\rho^{-2}Y{}_{i}{}_{j}(\gamma{}^{a}){}^{\alpha}{}^{\beta}(\gamma{}_{a}{}_{b}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\gamma}\big(\mathcal D^{b}\chi{}_{\beta}{}^{j}\big)\lambda{}_{\gamma}{}^{i} -\frac{1}{96}\rho^{-2}Y{}_{i}{}_{j}(\gamma{}_{a}){}^{\alpha}{}^{\beta}(\gamma{}_{b}{}_{c}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\gamma}R(Q){}^{b}{}^{c}{}_{\beta}{}^{j}\big(\mathcal D^{a}\lambda{}_{\gamma}{}^{i}\big)\\
&-\frac{i}{384}\rho^{-2}Y{}_{i}{}_{j}(\gamma{}_{a}{}_{b}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\beta}\epsilon{}_{c}{}^{a}{}^{b}{}^{d}{}^{e}R(Q){}_{d}{}_{e}{}^{\alpha}{}^{j}\big(\mathcal D^{c}\lambda{}_{\beta}{}^{i}\big) -\frac{1}{18}\rho^{-2}Y{}_{i}{}_{j}(\gamma{}^{a}){}^{\alpha}{}^{\beta}(\gamma{}_{b}{}_{a}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\gamma}\chi{}_{\beta}{}^{j}\big(\mathcal D^{b}\lambda{}_{\gamma}{}^{i}\big)\\
&+\frac{1}{6}\rho^{-2}Y{}_{i}{}_{j}(\gamma{}_{a}){}^{\alpha}{}^{\beta}\big(\mathcal D^{a}\chi{}_{\beta}{}^{j}\big)\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i} +\frac{5}{18}\rho^{-2}Y{}_{i}{}_{j}(\gamma{}_{a}){}^{\alpha}{}^{\beta}\chi{}_{\beta}{}^{j}\big(\mathcal D^{a}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\big)\\
&+\frac{i}{288}\rho^{-1}\big(\mathcal D^{a}R(V){}_{b}{}_{c}{}^{i}{}^{j}\big)Y{}_{j}{}_{i}(\gamma{}_{a}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\beta}(\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}){}_{\beta}{}^{\alpha} +\frac{1}{12}\rho^{-1}Y{}_{i}{}^{i}\delta{}_{\alpha}{}^{\beta}\mathsf C{}^{\gamma}{}^{\alpha}\mathsf C{}^{\delta}{}^{\rho}\mathsf C{}_{\gamma}{}_{\rho}\mathsf C{}_{\beta}{}_{\delta}\big(\mathcal D^{a}\mathcal D^{b}T{}_{b}{}_{a}\big)\\
&+\frac{1}{9}\rho^{-1}Y{}_{i}{}^{i}\delta{}_{\alpha}{}^{\beta}\mathsf C{}^{\gamma}{}^{\alpha}\mathsf C{}^{\delta}{}^{\rho}\mathsf C{}_{\gamma}{}_{\rho}\mathsf C{}_{\beta}{}_{\delta}T{}^{a}{}^{b}\big(\mathcal D^{c}T{}^{d}{}^{e}\big)\epsilon{}_{c}{}_{a}{}_{b}{}_{d}{}_{e} +\frac{1}{9}\rho^{-1}Y{}_{i}{}^{i}\delta{}_{\alpha}{}^{\beta}\mathsf C{}^{\gamma}{}^{\alpha}\mathsf C{}^{\delta}{}^{\rho}\mathsf C{}_{\gamma}{}_{\rho}\mathsf C{}_{\beta}{}_{\delta}T{}^{a}{}^{b}\big(\mathcal D^{c}T{}^{d}{}^{e}\big)\epsilon{}_{c}{}_{d}{}_{e}{}_{a}{}_{b}\\
&-\frac{i}{576}\rho^{-2}R(V){}_{a}{}_{b}{}^{i}{}^{j}Y{}_{j}{}_{i}(\gamma{}_{c}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\beta}(\gamma{}^{a}{}^{b}){}_{\beta}{}^{\alpha}\big(\mathcal D^{c}\rho\big) -\frac{1}{24}\rho^{-2}Y{}_{i}{}^{i}\delta{}_{\alpha}{}^{\beta}\mathsf C{}^{\gamma}{}^{\alpha}\mathsf C{}^{\delta}{}^{\rho}\mathsf C{}_{\gamma}{}_{\rho}\mathsf C{}_{\beta}{}_{\delta}\big(\mathcal D^{a}\rho\big)\big(\mathcal D^{b}T{}_{b}{}_{a}\big)\\
